### [1] [A Pythia8 Tune for Open Charm and Beauty Production in Fixed-Target Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29076)
*Matei Climescu,Didar Dobur,Kirill Skovpen*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了FTFT（Fixed-Target Fragmentation Tune），一套针对固定靶碰撞中粲夸克与底夸克强子产额优化的Pythia8参数集，适用于质心能量√s ≈ 20–42 GeV，服务于SHiP等束流倾倒实验对中微子通量与隐粒子产额的精确预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确模拟固定靶条件下重味强子（尤其是D介子）产生是预测束流倾倒实验（如SHiP）中中微子通量和隐粒子产额的关键需求。

Method: 采用两阶段拟合策略：(1) 利用π与p束固定靶实验测得的D介子Feynman-x (x_F)、横动量平方(p_T²)微分分布及电荷/领头粒子不对称性数据，拟合charm碎片化与多部分子相互作用（MPI）参数；(2) 基于各束流的包含粲夸克总截面测量值提取K因子，对模拟截面进行归一化标定。

Result: 获得了一组显著偏离LHC标准调参（如Monash或CP5）的FTFT参数，尤其在charm碎片函数与MPI强度上体现固定靶能区特有的动力学特征，并成功复现了D介子的x_F、p_T²分布与电荷不对称性。

Conclusion: FTFT为√s ~ 20–42 GeV固定靶重味物理提供了首个系统性、数据驱动的Pythia8调参方案，显著提升SHiP等实验背景与信号模拟的可靠性，亦为QCD碎裂机制的能标依赖研究提供关键约束。

Abstract: We present FTFT (Fixed-Target Fragmentation Tune), a set of Pythia8 parameters optimised for the production of charm and beauty hadrons in fixed-target collisions at centre-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s} \simeq 20-42$ GeV. Accurate heavy-flavour production in this regime is needed to predict neutrino fluxes and hidden-particle yields at beam-dump experiments such as SHiP. The tune is derived in two stages. First, differential distributions in Feynman-$x$ ($x_F$), transverse momentum squared ($p_T^2$), and charge or leading-particle production asymmetry of $D$ mesons are fitted to data from pion- and proton-beam fixed-target experiments. Second, $K$-factors for each beam are extracted from inclusive charm cross-section measurements to scale the simulated cross section to the measured data. The fitted charm-fragmentation and multiparton-interaction parameters depart from standard LHC-tuned defaults, in line with earlier fixed-target studies.

</details>


### [2] [Sensitivity of a Missing-Mass Search for a Light Dark Photon with a Positron Beam on a Hydrogen Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29159)
*Weizhi Xiong,Ashot Gasparian,Bogdan Wojtsekhowski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于Geant4模拟，研究利用JLab低能正电子束打液氢靶、结合前向高分辨电磁量能器探测末态光子，通过e⁺e⁻→γA′湮灭飞行过程的缺失质量法，探测轻暗光子A′，其灵敏度不依赖A′衰变模式；背景来自QED湮灭与轫致辐射；500 MeV束流下可覆盖mA′ = 7–19 MeV，ε²灵敏度达4×10⁻⁸（mA′ = 19 MeV）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持Jefferson Lab低能正电子设施的研发，探索轻暗光子这一热门BSM物理信号，尤其关注其非衰变依赖型探测方法

Method: Geant4蒙特卡洛模拟；正电子束轰击液氢靶诱发e⁺e⁻ → γA′湮灭-in-flight过程；采用缺失质量技术重建A′；前向高分辨率电磁量能器精确测量末态光子能量与动量

Result: 在500 MeV束流下，对mA′ ∈ [7, 19] MeV区间给出ε²的投影灵敏度，最弱耦合极限为ε² ≈ 4×10⁻⁸（对应mA′ = 19 MeV）；系统评估了QED本底（如e⁺e⁻ → γγ、e⁺ → e⁺γ）的压低能力

Conclusion: 该实验构型具备在MeV尺度上以无衰变依赖方式高效探查暗光子的能力，填补了现有实验在中低质量区的参数空间，为未来JLab Positron Facility提供了关键物理依据

Abstract: We present a Geant4-based simulation study of the sensitivity to a light dark photon, $A'$, using a positron beam incident on a thin liquid-hydrogen target and a high-resolution forward electromagnetic calorimeter for final-state photon reconstruction. This study is motivated by ongoing research and development toward a low-energy positron facility at Jefferson Lab. The proposed search employs the missing-mass technique in the annihilation-in-flight process $e^+e^- \to γA'$, providing sensitivity to the $A'$ independently of its decay mode. We evaluate the expected backgrounds from standard QED annihilation and bremsstrahlung processes and present the projected sensitivity to the kinetic-mixing parameter $ε^2$ as a function of the $A'$ mass. The experiment with 500~MeV beam will provide the data for $A'$ mass range 7-19 MeV. The projected sensitivity on $ε^2$ is $4\times10^{-8}$ at $m_{A'}=19$~MeV.

</details>


### [3] [Evidence for the dead-cone effect in bottom quark initiated jets produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29412)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中采集的数据，首次在微分层面观测到底夸克喷注中胶子辐射角分布对死锥效应的敏感依赖；通过迭代去簇化分析子喷注角分离分布，发现包含死锥效应的强子化蒙特卡洛模型与数据一致，而忽略该效应的模型被以4.3σ显著性排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验量子色动力学（QCD）中重夸克质量对胶子辐射的抑制效应——即死锥效应——在真实喷注结构中的可观测性，弥补理论预言与实验验证之间的空白。

Method: 基于59.8 fb⁻¹的√s = 13 TeV pp碰撞数据，选取tt̄事例中产生的b-喷注（p_T ∈ [40, 200] GeV），采用迭代去簇化（SoftDrop/ungrooming）提取子喷注对的角分离ΔR，并构造微分发射密度dN/dΔR作为观测量；与Pythia8和Herwig7等含/不含死锥修正的Parton Shower模型进行对比。

Result: 观测到明显的低ΔR辐射压低现象；含死锥效应的PS模型与数据在全ΔR区间一致；不含死锥的模型在ΔR < 0.2区域显著高估辐射，χ²检验给出4.3σ排斥置信度。

Conclusion: 首次在微分喷注子结构层面直接证实了QCD死锥效应的存在，为重夸克强子化建模、喷注校准及新物理背景估计提供了关键基准。

Abstract: The suppression of collinear gluon emissions from a massive quark, or the ``dead-cone effect,'' is the primary manifestation of quark mass effects in parton showers. A proton-proton collision data set recorded by the CMS experiment at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV with an integrated luminosity of 59.8 fb$^{-1}$ is analyzed. A differential measurement is presented of the emission density of bottom quark jets, studied as a function of the angular separation between iteratively declustered subjets. The measurement is performed in bottom quark initiated jets with transverse momenta between 40 and 200 GeV originating from top quark pair production. Compared to data, a parton shower Monte Carlo model that includes the dead-cone effect is favored, while a model without the dead-cone effect is disfavored with a significance of 4.3 standard deviations.

</details>

Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: XENONnT实验首次在暗物质探测器中实现低能太阳中微子（pp中微子）的弹性中微子-电子散射测量，能量阈值降至17 keV，达到迄今最低；以5.0σ显著性观测到pp中微子信号，通量为(10.2±2.0)×10¹⁰ cm⁻² s⁻¹，与Borexino结果在1.9σ内一致；标志着液氙时间投影室正式进入keV级中微子物理新阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展液氙暗物质探测器的多物理目标能力，验证其对亚100 keV太阳中微子的灵敏度，填补低能中微子探测空白，并为未来多功能中微子-暗物质联合观测站奠基。

Method: 利用XENONnT前两次科学运行共2.46 t·y曝光量，通过弹性中微子-电子散射（ν + e⁻ → ν + e⁻）在1–140 keV电子反冲能区提取pp太阳中微子信号；采用精细背景建模、脉冲形状甄别与多重屏蔽策略压制放射性本底。

Result: 首次在液氙探测器中以5.0σ显著性观测到pp太阳中微子；测得通量为(10.2 ± 2.0) × 10¹⁰ cm⁻² s⁻¹；能量阈值达17 keV，为当前所有中微子实验最低；同时确认CEνNS对⁸B中微子的观测兼容性。

Conclusion: 液氙时间投影室已具备keV量级太阳中微子探测能力，兼具高靶质量、低阈值与强本底抑制优势，正从单一暗物质搜寻平台演进为新一代中微子物理与粒子天体物理多用途观测平台。

Abstract: We report on the first measurement of low-energy solar neutrinos through elastic neutrino-electron scattering in a dark matter experiment, establishing the lowest energy threshold for any neutrino detection to date. The measurement utilizes data from the first two science runs of XENONnT, corresponding to an exposure of 2.46 t $\cdot$ y, and covers electron recoil energies between 1 keV and 140 keV, providing sensitivity to solar neutrinos with energies down to 17 keV. We reject the background-only hypothesis with a statistical significance of $5.0σ$ and measure a solar $pp$ neutrino flux of $(10.2 \pm 2.0) \times 10^{10}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$. The result is larger, but statistically consistent with the previous measurement by Borexino at $1.9σ$. Together with recent observations of coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering of $^8$B solar neutrinos in XENONnT and other liquid-xenon time projection chambers, these results demonstrate the growing potential of liquid xenon detectors for neutrino physics down to the keV-scale and represent an important milestone towards a next-generation multipurpose observatory.

</details>


### [5] [From a Long-standing Prediction to a New Family of Hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29502)
*Xining Wang,Kai Yi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了全粲四夸克态（fully charmed tetraquarks）从理论预言到LHC实验发现的发展历程，重点介绍NNU与THU团队在CMS实验中的关键贡献，包括该家族的发现、共振干涉现象的确立及首次量子数测定，标志着从单个奇特强子搜寻迈向系统性非微扰QCD研究的新阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用非微扰区域中多夸克态的形成机制与内部结构，检验QCD对奇特强子谱的预言能力。

Method: 结合高能对撞实验（CMS探测器数据）与理论模型分析（如共振参数拟合、角分布与自旋-宇称联合分析），辅以部分波分析与干涉效应建模。

Result: 发现多个全粲四夸克态（如X(6900)等）构成的共振家族；首次观测到相邻共振间的显著干涉效应；通过角动量耦合与衰变拓扑确定其J^PC = 0^{++}, 1^{+-}, 2^{++}等量子数组合。

Conclusion: 全粲四夸克态已形成可系统研究的强子新家族，其共振结构与量子数谱为色禁闭、动力学手征对称性破缺及强子分子态/紧致四夸克态图像提供了关键判据，成为探索QCD非微扰机制的理想探针。

Abstract: Fully charmed tetraquarks have evolved from a long-standing theoretical prediction into one of the most active frontiers in hadron spectroscopy. This article reviews their development from early theoretical studies and experimental searches to the recent breakthroughs at the Large Hadron Collider, with particular emphasis on the contributions of the Nanjing Normal University (NNU) and Tsinghua University (THU) teams at the CMS experiment. We summarize the discovery of the fully charmed tetraquark family, the establishment of interference among resonances, and the first determination of their quantum numbers. These advances have transformed the search for a single exotic resonance into the study of a new family of hadrons and opened a promising avenue for exploring the nonperturbative dynamics of Quantum Chromodynamics.

</details>

*Chenlei An,Dong Bai,Ziyu Bai,Kai Chen,Liangwen Chen,Xiang Chen,Jianqiao Deng,Yanxin Dou,Yicheng Feng,Zekai Feng,Lu Gao,Chang Gong,Aiqiang Guo,Liang Han,Qundong Han,Defu Hou,Ruiwen Hou,Huigang Hu,Chen Ji,Xiangdong Ji,Vijay Kumar,Dikai Li,Jiuzhao Li,Liang Li,Qite Li,Xin-Qiang Li,Yuan Li,Qiming Liang,Yutie Liang,Dong Liu,Duanqing Liu,Langtian Liu,Weijie Liu,Zejia Lu,Maowu Nie,Ziwen Pan,Hua Pei,Jinkang Peng,Shusu Shi,Qintao Song,Xiaocheng Song,Xiangming Sun,Yichen Sun,Zhiyu Sun,Enke Wang,Fei Wang,Hulin Wang,Jike Wang,Xiang-Peng Wang,Xiao Wang,Xin-Nian Wang,Yangxi Wang,Yaping Wang,Zeren Simon Wang,Yanbing Wei,Yuehong Xie,Weizhi Xiong,Nu Xu,Yu Xu,Siqi Yang,Yadong Yang,Hang Yin,Xing-Bo Yuan,Dongliang Zhang,Ranyu Zhang,Ruitian Zhang,Xueheng Zhang,Yu Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Zihan Zhao,Shuai Zhou,XiaoKang Zhou,Xiaoyu Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于HIAF高能重离子加速器建设的LUNE实验合作组，旨在利用高精度正负缪子束开展核子电磁结构、质子电荷半径疑难、QED检验及超出标准模型物理等前沿研究，分弹性散射与非弹性/结构探测两阶段推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决质子电荷半径疑难（proton charge radius puzzle），精确测定核子电磁形状因子以检验QED与强子相互作用，探索TMD、引力形状因子及核介质效应，并搜寻超出标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 依托HIAF提供的0.5–7.5 GeV/c高强度、高品质正负缪子束，开展缪子-质子弹性散射、缪子-核散射及深度非弹性过程；结合双电荷缪子束系统研究双光子交换效应；采用专用谱仪与高分辨率追踪/量能探测器实现动量与散射角精密测量。

Result: 预期将质子电荷半径测量精度提升至~1.0%；系统获取μ⁺/μ⁻散射截面比以约束两光子交换贡献；首次在缪子散射中提取核子TMD与引力形状因子；提供核物质中库仑畸变与介质效应的基准数据；对轻新粒子、暗光子等BSM信号设定新限制。

Conclusion: LUNE是连接低能电子散射与未来高能轻子-离子对撞机的关键桥梁，将显著拓展缪子作为探针在强子物理与基础对称性检验中的能力，确立中国在高精度缪子核物理领域的国际引领地位。

Abstract: The HIAF will provide high-intensity, high-quality muon beams with momenta from 0.5 to 7.5 GeV/c. This energy range is uniquely suited for precision muon scattering, bridging the gap between low-energy electron facilities and future high-energy lepton-ion colliders. In particular, HIAF will enable precision measurements with both positive and negative muon beams over a broad kinematic range, complementing existing electron-scattering facilities such as JLab, EicC and EIC.
  Based on HIAF muon source, the LUNE Collaboration has been established to address several fundamental questions in nuclear and particle physics, including the proton charge radius puzzle, nucleon electromagnetic structure, and the dynamics of quantum electrodynamics and hadronic interactions. The program proceeds in two phases, from elastic scattering to nucleon structure and beyond-Standard-Model searches.
  The experiment is expected to determine the proton charge radius with a precision of approximately 1.0\% using elastic muon-proton scattering. It will also perform systematic measurements of the proton electromagnetic form factors with both $μ^+$ and $μ^-$ beams, enabling precise studies of two-photon exchange effects and stringent tests of quantum electrodynamics. Beyond elastic scattering, LUNE will investigate TMD, gravitational form factors, and nuclear charge radii, providing new insights into the 3D structure of nucleons and nuclei. The experiment will further address important topics including Coulomb-distortion corrections, nuclear medium effects, and possible signatures of physics beyond the Standard Model.
  This white paper presents the scientific motivation, detector concept, expected performance, and long-term strategy of LUNE.

</details>

Yu,T. Yuan,S. Yun-C{á}rcamo,A. Zander Jurowitzki,A. Zegarelli,S. Zhang,Z. Zhang,P. Zhelnin,P. Zilberman,C. Zilleruelo Ca{ñ}as*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用IceCube观测到的高能中微子数据，对低能标引力理论（如大额外维模型和标准模型多重复制场景）施加了新的约束，特别是在0.5–100 TeV能区通过地球物质效应引起的谱形畸变进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决粒子物理中的等级问题，探索低能标引力理论在中微子物理中的可观测效应。

Method: 基于IceCube 10.7年向上μ子中微子数据（0.5–100 TeV），分析地球传播中微子的物质效应导致的能谱畸变，结合大额外维（LED）与多重标准模型（SM copies）理论框架进行参数拟合与置信度限制。

Result: 对大额外维模型，得到最大额外维紧致化半径上限为 R ≲ 0.17 μm（90% CL，正/反质量顺序均适用）；对多重SM复制模型，获得复制数下限 N ≳ O(400)，依赖最轻中微子质量；部分参数区域给出当前最强或首次探测的约束。

Conclusion: IceCube高能中微子数据为低能标引力新物理提供了独特探针，显著拓展并强化了对大额外维与SM多重复制模型的实验限制。

Abstract: The hierarchy problem remains an open question in particle physics. A number of theories that address this problem lower the fundamental scale of gravity, resulting in observable consequences in the neutrino sector. In this work, we place constraints on low-scale gravity scenarios using high-energy neutrinos observed with the IceCube Neutrino Observatory. The analysis is based on 10.7 years of upward-going muon neutrino data in the energy range from 0.5 to 100 TeV. In this energy range, the theories predict characteristic spectral distortions arising from matter effects when neutrinos propagate through Earth. In the context of large extra dimension models, we constrain the compactification radius of the largest extra dimension to $R \lesssim 0.17\,μ\mathrm{m}$ at $90\%$ confidence level for both normal and inverted neutrino mass ordering. For scenarios with multiple Standard Model copies, we obtain lower limits of up to $N \gtrsim \mathcal{O}(400)$, depending on the value of the lightest neutrino mass. In parts of the parameter space, these results constitute the strongest constraints in the literature to our knowledge, while in other regions they probe previously unexplored parameter space.

</details>


### [8] [ConCA: Concentration-Aware Channel Attention for Fine-Grained Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30183)
*Yu-Sheng Liu,Yu-Chen Tung*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的轻量级通道注意力机制ConCA，通过引入负输入熵（NegEnt）来刻画通道激活的空间集中度，与全局平均池化（GAP）构成双描述符，并采用深度可分离1D卷积MLP生成通道权重，在细粒度视觉识别（FGVR）任务中显著优于SE-Net、ECA-Net等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级通道注意力机制（如SE、ECA）依赖全局平均池化（GAP）单一描述通道激活强度，忽略空间分布差异；而细粒度识别对空间敏感性高，需同时建模幅度与集中度。

Method: ConCA构造双通道描述符：(1) GAP表征激活幅度；(2) 基于负激活softmax的平移不变负熵（NegEnt）表征空间集中度；二者拼接后经深度可分离1D卷积MLP（参数量O(C)）映射为通道权重。

Result: 在6个细粒度基准上超越无注意力、SE-Net、ECA-Net及4种更复杂描述符模块；在iNat2021-mini上跨8种骨干网络验证泛化性。

Conclusion: 通道描述符的设计（幅度+集中度）及其到注意力权重的映射机制，是轻量级通道注意力在FGVR中性能提升的关键未被充分探索维度。

Abstract: Lightweight channel attention mechanisms are widely used in image classification, yet their effectiveness in fine-grained visual recognition (FGVR) remains limited. Most modules summarize each channel by global average pooling (GAP), which captures activation magnitude but ignores spatial concentration, so channels with different spatial distributions but identical means receive the same descriptor. We propose Concentration-Aware Channel Attention (ConCA), which pairs the mean with a shift-invariant negative-input entropy (NegEnt), computed via a softmax over the negated activations, forming a dual descriptor that jointly encodes magnitude and concentration. A depthwise 1-D convolutional multi-layer perceptron (MLP), whose parameter count is linear in the number of channels, maps the pair to a per-channel weight. On six fine-grained benchmarks, ConCA improves over attention-free, SE-Net, and ECA-Net baselines as well as four richer descriptor-based modules under a controlled from-scratch protocol, and it generalizes across eight backbones on iNat2021-mini. These results indicate that the channel descriptor, together with the per-channel gating that maps it to attention weights, is an important but underexplored aspect of lightweight channel attention in FGVR.

</details>

Girgus,P. Govindaraj,M. Posiadala-Zezula,Y. S. Prabhu,S. B. Boyd,R. Edwards,D. Hadley,M. O'Flaherty,B. Richards,A. Ali,B. Jamieson,C. Bronner,D. Horiguchi,A. Minamino,Y. Sasaki*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用Super-Kamiokande探测器纯水相401 kton·年的曝光量，对质子衰变模式p→l⁺+X（l⁺=e⁺或μ⁺，X为不可见、无质量、中性粒子）开展搜寻，未发现超出大气中微子本底的显著信号，从而设定了该衰变道90%置信度下的部分寿命下限：p→e⁺+X为1.72×10³³年，p→μ⁺+X为0.61×10³³年，分别比先前结果提高2倍和1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大统一理论（GUTs）及超越标准模型物理所预言的质子不稳定性，特别是含轻子数守恒破缺与暗区耦合的新机制（如p→l⁺+X，X可能为暗光子、标量或惰性中微子等轻暗物质候选者）。

Method: 基于Super-Kamiokande纯水切伦科夫探测器的全部运行数据（401 kton·年曝光量），采用蒙特卡洛模拟精确建模大气中微子本底与信号事例的径迹拓扑、能量沉积与时间结构，通过多变量分析与似然拟合，在e⁺与μ⁺末态通道中分别提取信号区间并设定寿命下限。

Result: 在90% CL下获得p→e⁺+X的部分寿命下限为1.72×10³³年，p→μ⁺+X为0.61×10³³年；未观测到统计显著性>3σ的超背景信号；本底拟合与系统误差（如中微子截面、光学模型、能量刻度）均被严格约束。

Conclusion: 当前数据不支持p→l⁺+X类衰变在所探能区存在，对含轻暗物质耦合的GUT/超对称/额外维模型施加了迄今最强实验约束，同时验证了Super-K探测器对单轻子+缺失能量末态的高灵敏分辨能力。

Abstract: A search for proton decay via $p\rightarrow l^{+}+X$, where $l^{+}$ is a positively charged lepton and $X$ is an invisible, massless, neutral particle, was performed using a 401~kton$\cdot$years exposure representing the entire pure water phase of Super-Kamiokande. No significant indication of a proton decay was observed beyond the expected atmospheric neutrino background. Lower limits on the partial lifetime of the proton were set to at $1.72\times10^{33}$ years for $p\rightarrow e^{+}+X$ and $0.61\times10^{33}$ years for $p\rightarrow μ^{+}+X$ at the $90\%$ confidence level. These results improve on previous limits by factors of 2 and 1.5, respectively.

</details>

Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文测量了质子-氩（pAr）和质子-质子（pp）碰撞中ψ(2S)与J/ψ产生截面比，发现pAr中该比值相对于pp显著降低（0.90±0.04±0.02），表明存在核效应，为LHCb后续重核系统研究提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子化过程中核物质对粲偶素（特别是激发态ψ(2S)）产生的修饰作用，即核效应（如初始态能量损失、终态吸收、色荷屏蔽等），需以小系统（pA）为基准对比pp参考。

Method: 利用LHCb实验在SMOG2储气室中注入氩气/氢气实现固定靶pAr/pp碰撞，中心系能量√s_NN=113 GeV；通过双μ子衰变道重建ψ(2S)与J/ψ，差分测量其截面比随横动量p_T与中心系快度y*的分布，并作pAr/pp比值分析。

Result: 在-2.3<y*<0.0且0<p_T<8 GeV/c区间，pAr中ψ(2S)/J/ψ截面比相对于pp的比值为0.90±0.04(stat)±0.02(syst)，显著低于1，表明ψ(2S)在核环境中相对压低。

Conclusion: 该结果首次在LHCb固定靶pAr系统中明确观测到ψ(2S)相对J/ψ的抑制，证实轻核已显现核效应，为未来PbPb或XeXe等更大核系统中区分冷核物质效应与夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）热效应提供了关键基准。

Abstract: A measurement of the $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ production cross-section ratio is performed in proton-argon ($p\mathrm{Ar}$) and proton-proton ($pp$) collisions in fixed-target mode at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=113\,\mathrm{GeV}$. Data samples were collected by the LHCb experiment during argon and hydrogen gas injections in the SMOG2 storage cell, resulting in $p\mathrm{Ar}$ and $pp$ collisions, respectively. The $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ production cross-section ratio is measured as a function of the charmonium transverse momentum, $p_{\mathrm{T}}$, and rapidity in the centre-of-mass system, $y^{*}$. The $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ ratio in $p\mathrm{Ar}$ collisions over that in $pp$ collisions is measured to be $0.90 \pm 0.04 \pm 0.02$ for $-2.3<y^{*}<0.0$ and $0<p_{\mathrm{T}}<8\mathrm{GeV}/c$, indicating the emergence of nuclear effects in the $p\mathrm{Ar}$ system. This study acts as a baseline for the interpretation of future measurements with larger systems accessible by the LHCb experiment.

</details>

### [11] [Reimagining Gravity: Generalized Symmetries, Double Copy, and Spinning Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28736)
*Joon-Hwi Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过三种不同视角探讨广义相对论中的三个开放问题：(1) 动力学引力中是否存在广义对称性；(2) 引力与杨-米尔斯理论树图双拷贝关系的场论解释；(3) 四维自旋黑洞在外部场中的精确运动方程与有效拉氏量。分别基于洛伦兹群一形式对称性、微分同胚规范结构与自对偶/反自对偶分解展开研究，并将纽曼-贾尼斯算法提升为严格导出，给出自对偶黑洞度规及自旋黑洞康普顿振幅的手征拉氏量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代广义相对论研究揭示了三个核心问题：动力学引力中广义对称性的存在性、双拷贝关系的场论基础、以及四维自旋黑洞在外部场中精确有效描述的缺失。这些问题挑战了传统时空与自旋观念，亟需新视角重构引力理论框架。

Method: 采用三重互补方法：(I) 将引力视为洛伦兹群规范理论，构建动力学引力中的一形式对称性；(II) 从微分同胚群规范结构出发，关联色-动量对偶与双拷贝；(III) 将四维引力分解为自对偶与反自对偶扇区，发展手征形式论，并结合经典双拷贝与点粒子有效理论推导黑洞解与动力学。

Result: 1. 建立了动力学引力中洛伦兹一形式对称性；2. 揭示Kerr度规可严格分解为一对自对偶/反自对偶Taub-NUT解，使Newman-Janis算法获得严格导出地位；3. 提出动态探针版Newman-Janis算法；4. 给出自旋黑洞康普顿振幅的拉氏量推导，具备正确因子化性质且无虚假极点，同时建立最大化利用自对偶简化的手征形式论。

Conclusion: 引力可通过洛伦兹对称性、微分同胚规范结构与自对偶分解三重透镜统一理解；双拷贝不仅是计算工具，更是深层几何与对称结构的体现；自对偶性为构造自旋黑洞精确有效理论提供了自然手征框架，有望弥合量子散射与经典黑洞物理之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: General relativity is a century-old subject. Yet modern explorations through generalized symmetries, scattering amplitudes, and effective field theory have raised open problems, motivating a reassessment of conventional views on gravitation, spacetime, and spin. First, do generalized symmetries exist in dynamical gravity as a low-energy EFT? Second, is there a field-theoretic explanation for the tree-level double copy relationship between general relativity and Yang-Mills theory? Third, what are the exact equations of motion or Lagrangians of four-dimensional spinning black holes in external fields, in point-particle effective theory? In this dissertation, three different perspectives on gravity are developed to shed light on each of these puzzles.
  In Part I, we view gravity as a gauge theory of Lorentz group to establish a one-form symmetry of dynamical gravity. In Part II, we investigate how far gravity can be viewed as a gauge theory of diffeomorphisms, in connection with color-kinematics duality and double copy. In Part III, we view four-dimensional gravity as a nonlinear interaction between self-dual and anti-self-dual parts. We apply this view to the derivation of four-dimensional spinning black hole solutions as well as their dynamics in point-particle effective theory. First, we derive a new self-dual black hole metric from classical double copy and show that the Kerr metric represents a pair of self-dual and anti-self-dual Taub-NUT solutions, elevating the Newman-Janis algorithm to a rigorous derivation. Second, we propose a dynamical probe counterpart of Newman-Janis algorithm. We give a Lagrangian derivation of spinning black hole Compton amplitudes that exhibit correct factorizations and are free of spurious poles for all helicity configurations while developing a chiral formalism that manifests and maximally utilizes the simplicity of the self-dual sector.

</details>


### [12] [Carroll-Cotton Tensors and Gravitational Radiation at Null Infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28741)
*Adrien Fiorucci,Simon Pekar,P. Marios Petropoulos,Matthieu Vilatte*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过规范共形Carroll代数，首次从第一性原理定义了共形Carroll几何上的Cotton张量；该张量既是刻画Carroll几何共形非平坦性的共形协变量，也是描述四维渐近平直时空共形边界上引力辐射自由度的天然几何工具，相较于需额外Geroch张量修正的Bondi news，Carroll-Cotton张量更自然地表征由引力波辐射引起的非定态偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Extending a previous data-driven scheme for two-body systems, we apply symbolic regression (PySR) to uncover universal three-body FVE formulae. Abstract: We study the existence of gravitational radiation at infinity for pure-radiation Robinson-Trautman metrics with one spacelike Killing vector and a cosmological constant $Λ$ of arbitrary sign. For $Λ\neq 0$, we use criteria for the existence of gravitational radiation based on the asymptotic super-Poynting vector. For point sources, we extend to $Λ\neq 0$ the known $Λ=0$ result that the Kinnersley rocket is the only rocket without gravitational radiation. However, for string and sheet sources, new possibilities without gravitational radiation arise. For $Λ>0$, we show that the vanishing of the canonical asymptotic super-Poynting vector---computed with respect to the unit normal to scri---exactly characterizes the absence of radiation. On the other hand, for $Λ<0$, we show that the appropriate criterion is the vanishing of the components normal to scri of the asymptotic super-Poynting vectors associated with any unit timelike vector tangent to scri or, equivalently, the proportionality between the Cotton-York tensor and the holographic stress tensor at scri. In particular, we provide explicit examples of metrics for which the Cotton-York and holographic stress tensors commute, yet gravitational radiation is present because the two tensors are not proportional. We also determine the principal null directions (PNDs) of the rescaled Weyl tensor at scri for both signs of $Λ$ and relate their geometry relative to the normal to scri to the tensorial criteria for gravitational radiation.

Abstract: Strong magnetic fields affect particle dynamics through two distinct channels: gravitational backreaction deforms the spacetime, while direct coupling to an intrinsic magnetic moment depends on the relative orientation of the field and the dipole. We disentangle these effects by studying equatorial orbits and near-horizon collisions of electrically neutral magnetized particles in the exact Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson spacetime. The field-induced geometric deformation shifts turning points and circular-orbit domains and can eliminate a finite effective-potential well together with its bound orbits. Even without direct dipole coupling, it can also offset the Kerr periapsis advance and produce a finite-radius zero-precession orbit. Direct dipole coupling breaks the symmetry under magnetic-field reversal and shifts the radius, energy, and angular momentum of the innermost stable circular orbit in an orientation-dependent manner. The formal ultrarelativistic endpoints of these orbit branches, however, remain fixed by the background geometry and approach circular null orbits. In the Bañados--Silk--West mechanism, both magnetic effects modify finite-radius potential barriers and hence the ability of a critical particle to reach the near-horizon collision region. In the representative nonzero-field cases examined here, an exactly critical particle released from infinity is blocked before reaching an extremal horizon, although a locally admissible collision between critical and usual particles can still produce unbounded center-of-mass energy. Finite dipole coupling shifts the barriers but does not change the leading near-horizon divergence. Near a nonextremal horizon, an exactly critical particle is excluded and the collision energy remains finite.

Abstract: Black holes link gravity, thermodynamics and information via limits on the relaxation rate of a perturbed system. The Bekenstein-Hod bound imposes a minimum relaxation time at fixed temperature, but its observational test demands both black hole thermodynamic characterization and decay time measurement. Here we test this bound with GW250114, the loudest gravitational-wave signal yet observed from a binary black-hole merger. We infer the remnant temperature from pre-merger data truncated at least $10\,M$ before the peak and its longest-lived decay time from post-merger data, thereby avoiding direct reuse of the same strain samples. For the primary $t_{<}=-10\,M$ analysis, the bound is verified at $3.3-3.6σ$ across the focal ringdown start times, representing a substantial improvement over the $91\%$ confidence level set by GW150914. Abstract: Traversable wormholes in general relativity require non-standard matter sources, making the identification of physically motivated equations of state particularly important. We investigate wormholes supported by a polytropic equation of state, considering homogeneous and inhomogeneous configurations within a unified framework. We derive the corresponding solutions and analyze the effects of the polytropic parameters on the geometry and energy conditions. In the homogeneous case, the polytropic construction yields a consistent wormhole interior whose geometry and matter content are governed by the constant polytropic parameters. For the inhomogeneous case, we obtain a general analytical expression showing that the geometry is completely determined by the radial polytropic coefficient $ω(r)$. For positive $ω(r)$, the requirement for physically meaningful solutions naturally restricts the polytropic exponent to odd integer values. Using a power-law profile, we construct explicit classes of solutions exhibiting distinct parameter regimes and finite radial support. Interestingly enough, for an exponent $α=2γ-3$, a generalized absurdly benign traversable wormhole-like configuration emerges naturally. Although the flare-out condition implies null-energy-condition violation at the throat, the inhomogeneous framework allows its radial distribution to be controlled. Our results establish a systematic connection between polytropic matter and wormhole geometry, providing a flexible framework for constructing compact wormholes with localized exotic matter.

Abstract: Nambu-Goto cosmic string loops generically develop cusps, points where the string momentarily reaches the speed of light. These cusps produce strong gravitational wave bursts with a characteristic strain spectrum $\tilde{h}(ω)\propto (Gμ)\,ω^{-4/3}$, making them prime targets for gravitational wave searches, where $μ$ is the string mass per unit length. However, this picture is modified when one accounts for gravitational backreaction. Using a convenient gauge, we reformulate the Nambu-Goto equations of motion for a loop moving in its own dynamically sourced gravitational field, enabling the first continuous numerical evolution of loops under this backreaction. A key finding is that locally cusps survive backreaction. Nevertheless, the gravitational waveform calculated in this formalism is significantly modified, weakening the cusp burst and introducing a high-frequency cutoff at $f_c\propto(Gμ)^{-3/2}$. This suppression above $f_c$ reduces the expected signal-to-noise ratio in current and near-future detectors relative to unperturbed waveform predictions.

Abstract: We define a relative $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index $\mathcal{N}(ω_1,ω_2)$ for locally-comparable, parity-invariant pure states on a unital $C^*$-algebra. For the one-dimensional self-dual CAR algebra, we apply this construction to the half-chain automorphism $σ$ and define the many-body Majorana number $\mathcal{N}σ(ω):=\mathcal{N}(ω,ω\circσ)$ for parity-invariant $σ$-local pure states. In the quasi-free Hilbert--Schmidt regime, this index agrees with the usual single-body Majorana number. We then introduce symmetric local automorphism paths and prove that $\mathcal{N}_σ$ completely classifies the resulting automorphic-path-components. Abstract: Pseudorandom structures are structures that behave like random ones, without necessarily being random themselves. We show that $V$ is pseudorandom in terms of its diffraction properties, that is, the associated diffraction measure is purely absolutely continuous for all $λ\not= 0$, all irrational $α$, and all $x_1,x_2$ --- which is the case as well for the random case. Conclusion: 欧拉框架下Kraichnan模型的高精度模拟不仅可复现全部已知Lagrangian统计特征，还能揭示增量分布的精细非普适结构；参数标度律的明确建立使该方法成为拓展至非白噪声、耦合混沌等现实湍流模型的可信数值平台。

Abstract: We present a systematic Eulerian study of passive scalar advection in rough two-dimensional Kraichnan flows, covering the full range of velocity roughness exponent $h\in(0,1)$. The advection--diffusion equation is integrated directly using a pseudo-spectral method at resolutions up to $2048^2$ grid points, with a frozen-noise Runge--Kutta scheme consistent with the white-in-time construction of the carrier flow. The simulations recover the duality between advected scalar and advecting flow---the smoother the carrier, the rougher the scalar---together with the predicted scaling laws for the second-order statistics. Once the scaling range is properly identified, the fourth-order flatness anomaly is measured across the whole range of $h$, in agreement with the Lagrangian estimates of Frisch \textit{et al.} (1999) and with the perturbative predictions of Bernard \textit{et al.} (1998) and Pumir \textit{et al.} (1997). Benefiting from the fact that our Eulerian simulations give direct access to the full scalar field, we also examine the probability density functions of scalar increments and the corresponding higher-order statistics, which show systematic departures from Gaussianity and from log-normality, with the strongest deviations manifesting for intermediate values of $h$. A central outcome of this work is a systematic account of how the simulation parameters, in particular the molecular diffusivity, must be adjusted with $h$, providing practical guidelines for reliable simulations; we further show that the residual deviations from the theoretical scaling laws are quantitatively accounted for by the finite spectral representation of the carrier flow. Our analysis also serve as a numerical baseline for simulating more realistic extensions of the Kraichnan model, where the carrier flow is coupled with a Gaussian multiplicative chaos and theoretical developments are limited.

Abstract: Dynamical localization requires all position moments of a quantum wavepacket to remain bounded in time, but for quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators such bounds are generally not uniform in phase. In the positive Lyapunov exponent regime, the best known phase-uniform estimates instead grow on a logarithmic scale. We prove that both the logarithmic scale and the dependence on the moment order are sharp for a class of one-frequency quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators with even potentials. Our main ingredient is a reflective version of semi-uniformly localized eigenfunctions, adapted to the two localization centers forced by a completely resonant phase, from which we obtain matching logarithmic lower bounds along sequences of times.

The input data depend on (n+1) variables and are, therefore, formally determined ones. Stability estimates and uniqueness theorem are obtained for this inverse problem. In addition, the initial condition is assumed to be known, except of two publications cited in the text.

Abstract: Strong topological insulators are classified by integer invariants which admit different real-space expressions. Inspired by recent work studying these invariants for spaces with curved boundaries, we revisit the problem of equivalence of the various expressions, including Fredholm index pairings and Kubo formulas involving half-space projections. Using Roe algebras and a variant of KK-theory suitable for non-separable $C^*$-algebras, we prove a general form of bulk-edge correspondence for the index pairing with a general position-space Dirac operator in the presence of arbitrary boundaries. For spaces coarsely equivalent to $\mathbb{R}^d$, we show that up to multiplicity, these pairings are equal to those obtained with the standard dual Dirac operator, with the multiplicity explicitly given as the topological degree of the symbol function. In addition, we prove a generalized Kubo formula which computes the general index pairing by a real-space formula.

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for implicit discrete Hamiltonian systems based on discrete Morse families, Lagrangian relations, and discrete analogues of Tulczyjew's triple. The main idea is to regard the Lagrangian submanifold defining the discrete dynamics, rather than an explicit symplectic evolution map, as the fundamental geometric object. This viewpoint naturally accommodates implicit, constrained, and degenerate discrete systems.
  Within this framework, we formulate a Type--II discrete Hamilton--Jacobi theory in terms of the propagation of Lagrangian submanifolds between consecutive discrete steps. When these submanifolds are locally represented by exact one-forms $dW_k$ and $dW_{k+1}$, the resulting equations provide a discrete Hamilton--Jacobi relation between consecutive generating functions. More generally, when the projection onto configuration space becomes singular and a single-valued generating function ceases to exist, we show that the evolution can be described by the composition of Type--II discrete dynamics with Morse families. This yields a generating family for the propagated Lagrangian submanifold without requiring the dynamics to be represented as a graph.
  As an application, we consider the propagation of optical wavefronts through fold caustics. A Type--II discrete Hamiltonian yields a symplectic ray integrator, while a Morse family represents the multivalued wavefront near the caustic. Their composition provides a discrete propagation rule for the complete Lagrangian manifold across the singularity. In this way, the same geometric construction simultaneously provides a discrete Hamiltonian integrator and a regular representation of multivalued Hamilton--Jacobi solutions.

Abstract: The chiral limit of the Bistritzer--MacDonald model for twisted bilayer graphene has exactly flat bands at magic angles. We show that exact flatness can persist beyond the chiral limit with nonchiral tunnelling potentials in the usual symmetry class. We construct a family of nonchiral tunnelling potentials for which the Hamiltonian has at least two zero-energy states at every Bloch momentum and for every real value of the nonchiral coupling. This gives a family of counterexamples to Open Problem~3 of Zworski's survey \cite{ZworskiSurvey}. Conversely, we prove that every admissible potential with this property belongs to this family, thereby obtaining a complete characterization. By contrast, we show that the standard BM model, namely, BM Hamiltonians with first harmonic tunnelling potentials, does not admit exactly flat bands at the magic angle except possibly at a discrete set of nonchiral coupling strengths, with no finite accumulation point. To prove this, we introduce an explicit invertibility criterion. The criterion requires the projected perturbation to have a nonzero scalar coefficient at some Bloch momentum, obstructing exact flatness for general nonchiral tunnelling. A local Taylor expansion argument verifies the criterion for the standard BM potentials. For the family constructed above, however, the projected perturbation vanishes at every Bloch momentum, so the invertibility criterion fails.

</details>

Abstract: Bayesian analyses of heavy-ion collisions rely on functional inputs, such as temperature-dependent transport coefficients and the equation of state, whose prior specification remains a significant source of uncertainty. Standard approaches employ low-dimensional parametrizations that can impose artificial correlations and leave the resulting inference sensitive to the assumed functional form.
  We develop a nonparametric framework for constructing priors over such functions using Gaussian processes, tailored to emulator-based inference in heavy-ion phenomenology. Truncated Karhunen--Loève expansions yield optimized finite-dimensional representations suitable for use as emulator inputs. We investigate how physical constraints can be incorporated into these priors, demonstrating that rejection-based methods produce non-Gaussian measures and induce nontrivial correlations in the expansion coefficients. By contrast, pushforward constructions enforce the constraints while preserving a tractable Gaussian measure in a latent space.
  These results provide a systematic framework for incorporating physical constraints into functional priors and clarify their effects on the statistical structure of the finite-dimensional representations used in Bayesian inference.

</details>

Abstract: Triangle singularities, predicted by Landau in 1959, are purely kinematic enhancements arising from hadronic rescattering loops. Despite their proposed role in various anomalous decay processes and exotic hadron candidates, a direct experimental confirmation has remained elusive for more than six decades. We analyze the $pη/\bar{p}η$ invariant mass spectrum in $ψ(2S) \to p\bar{p}η$ measured by the BESIII Collaboration. The data exhibit a clear cusp-like structure around 1.564~GeV in the $N(1535)$ region, in precise agreement with the kinematic position predicted for the triangle singularity. Including the triangle singularity loop in the fit substantially improves the description of the data, with $χ^2/\mathrm{d.o.f.}$ decreasing from 1.22 to 0.90, corresponding to a significance of $\sim 3.8σ$ for the triangle singularity contribution. The precise alignment of the observed excess with the predicted kinematic position provides the first compelling evidence for the triangle singularity effect.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光锥分布振幅（LCDAs）对精确唯象研究至关重要，而通过大动量有效理论（LaMET）从格点QCD提取LCDAs需依赖准分布振幅（quasi-DAs），但其因子化存在1/P_h幂次修正（含靶质量与高扭度效应），尤其对重子体系尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 基于矩关系建立quasi-DA与LCDA的严格联系，利用算符乘积展开（OPE）与重整化群分析，推导靶质量修正的全阶闭式解析表达式；结合格点数据对Λ重子进行数值评估，并显式构造下一领头扭度（twist-4）算符以完善quasi-DA因子化框架。

Result: 获得领头扭度下靶质量修正的全阶闭式关系，适用于任意质量重子及准横动量依赖分布（quasi-TMDs）；数值显示Λ重子中该修正随动量增大快速压低、端点区可忽略；明确给出twist-4算符的具体形式及其在quasi-DA因子化中的贡献结构。

Conclusion: 本文为重子quasi-DA的幂次修正提供了首个系统性理论框架，所获闭式关系与算符构造显著提升LaMET方法提取重子LCDAs的精度可控性，是迈向高精度格点重子结构研究的关键一步。

Abstract: Light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) are essential to precision phenomenological studies. They can be accessed from lattice QCD through the large-momentum effective theory (LaMET) via quasi-distribution amplitudes (quasi-DAs). Factorization of quasi-DAs receive power corrections in inverse powers of the hadron momentum, including target-mass and higher-twist corrections. In this work, we present the first systematic analysis of such power corrections for the leading-twist baryon quasi-DA. Establishing the moment relation between the quasi-DA and the LCDA, we derive an exact closed-form relation that resums target-mass correction to all orders at leading twist. This result also applies to heavy baryons and to quasi-transverse-momentum-dependent distributions. We numerically assess these corrections for the $Λ$ baryon quasi-DA using existing lattice data, finding that the target-mass correction decreases rapidly with increasing baryon momentum and is almost negligible in the endpoint regions. In addition, we explicitly construct the next-to-leading-twist operators entering the quasi-DA factorization. Our results are a first step toward quantifying the power corrections in future lattice determinations of light or heavy baryon LCDAs.

</details>


### [98] [Taming lepton portal dark matter by a non-invertible selection rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29051)
*Junichiro Kawamura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetry）作为选择定则，约束轻子门户暗物质模型中的味破坏效应，解决了传统可逆对称性（如U(1)或离散群Z_N）无法同时压制味破坏耦合与兼容PMNS大混合角的矛盾；构造了一个基于Z_5全局对称性经Z_2规范化的简单模型，实现单味主导的暗物质-轻子耦合，并讨论了I型跷跷板补全下的味泄漏及Z玻色子衰变中轻子味非普适性的实验探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可逆对称性无法在允许PMNS大混合角的同时压制轻子门户暗物质中的味破坏耦合，导致与直接探测实验（如XENON、LUX）和味物理观测（如μ→eγ、Z→ℓℓ′）冲突；需新机制协调味结构与暗物质唯象学。

Method: 引入非可逆选择定则——通过对Z_5全局对称性进行Z_2规范化，生成非可逆范畴对称性；该结构自然导出受拓扑缺陷约束的耦合选择规则，使暗物质仅与单一轻子味（如τ）强耦合，而其余味通过PMNS混合间接参与，从而压制树图级味破坏过程。

Result: 1) 证明Z_2 gauging of Z_5可实现one-flavor dominance；2) 该构造与PMNS矩阵兼容，且规避μ→eγ等强约束；3) 在type-I seesaw下，重右手中微子诱导的圈图产生可控味泄漏；4) Z→eμ、Z→eτ、Z→μτ衰变分支比可达10^{−7}–10^{−6}，为LHCb、Belle II及未来FCC-ee提供可测信号。

Conclusion: 非可逆对称性是协调轻子门户暗物质与味物理的关键新范式；其选择定则既保障暗物质弱耦合以逃逸直接探测，又天然抑制低能味破坏，同时预言Z玻色子味非普适衰变为关键检验渠道。

Abstract: We point out that a non-invertible selection rule can control the flavor violation inherent in lepton portal dark matter, a weakly interacting massive particle candidate that can evade severe constraints from direct detection experiments. We show that ordinary invertible symmetries cannot suppress flavor-violating portal couplings while accommodating the large mixing angles in the PMNS matrix. We then present a simple construction based on the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauging of a $\mathbb{Z}_5$ symmetry, which realizes one-flavor dominance of the portal couplings consistently with the PMNS mixing matrix. We also discuss possible flavor leakage in a type-I seesaw completion and study experimental probes of the model through lepton observables, especially lepton flavor non-universality in $Z$ boson decays.

</details>


### [99] [Higgs boson pair production in SMEFT: shape and truncation studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29132)
*Manosch Bär,Bakar Chargeishvili,Ramona Gröber,Gudrun Heinrich,Konstantin Schmid*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了SMEFT中维度-6算符（含圈压低项）的Wilson系数对双Higgs产生截面的影响，包含NLO QCD修正，并基于Wilson系数的多项式参数化分析微分分布（如$m_{hh}$和$p_{T,h}$），识别出若干导致显著形状畸变的基准场景；部分场景在仅保留线性SMEFT展开时仍物理合理，而另一些则因忽略二次维度-6贡献而给出非物理微分截面，揭示了大反常耦合下线性近似的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测超出标准模型（BSM）物理需高精度刻画EFT有效拉氏量中Wilson系数对可观测量的影响，尤其在双Higgs产生这一对新物理敏感但信噪比低的过程；需厘清线性SMEFT截断的适用边界，避免因忽略高阶项导致错误物理解释。

Method: 采用基于NLO QCD修正的、以Wilson系数为变量的多项式拟合形式表征双Higgs产生微分截面；系统扫描维度-6算符组合，聚焦$m_{hh}$不变质量谱与领头Higgs横动量$p_{T,h}$分布；通过比较线性截断与含二次项结果，判别各基准点的物理自洽性。

Result: 识别出若干使$m_{hh}$与$p_{T,h}$分布发生显著形状畸变的Wilson系数构型；其中部分构型在线性近似下仍给出正定微分截面，而另一些则在缺失二次维度-6贡献时出现负截面或非物理行为，定量标定了线性SMEFT在双Higgs道中的有效性阈值。

Conclusion: 双Higgs微分分布是约束SMEFT Wilson系数（尤其高阶项）的强有力探针；单纯依赖线性SMEFT展开可能导致严重偏差，尤其在反常耦合较强区域；未来实验分析必须纳入维度-6算符的二次贡献以确保理论预测的物理合理性与解释力。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of the influence of Wilson coefficients on Higgs boson pair production cross sections in SMEFT up to dimension-6, also including loop-suppressed operators. Our study accounts for NLO QCD corrections, and is based on updated parametrizations of the cross section as a polynomial in the Wilson coefficients. We focus on differential distributions for the Higgs boson pair invariant mass, as well as the transverse momenta of the leading Higgs bosons, identifying benchmark scenarios exhibiting pronounced shape distortions in the $m_{hh}$ and $p_{T,h}$ distributions. While some of these scenarios remain physically meaningful under a purely linear truncation of the SMEFT expansion, others yield unphysical differential cross sections when quadratic dimension-6 contributions are omitted, exposing the breakdown of the linear approximation for large anomalous couplings.

</details>


### [100] [Probing strange-sector flavor-changing neutral currents at DUNE-like facilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29178)
*Xin-Shuai Yan,Miao Liu,Qin Chang,Ya-Dong Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究DUNE类实验设施通过包含性深度非弹性中微子散射对下夸克-奇异夸克味改变中性流（FCNC）相互作用的探测灵敏度；在Dirac与Majorana中微子通用低能有效拉格朗日框架下，给出近/远探测器对无量纲Wilson系数（以费米常数GF为基准）的投影限制，近探测器达O(10⁻³–10⁻²)，远探测器弱约一个数量级；系统误差中，近探测器受背景率不确定主导，远探测器受信号率不确定主导；远探测器限制对中微子质量序及δCP的依赖主要局限于含电子味的系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测超出标准模型的味改变中性流（FCNC）过程，特别是d↔s跃迁，是检验新物理的重要探针；中微子束流实验（如DUNE）提供高统计、宽能谱、多味通道的独特平台。

Method: 基于通用低能有效场论（EFT）框架，构建含Dirac与Majorana中微子的FCNC耦合项；模拟DUNE近/远探测器几何与响应；采用CP-与τ-优化束流配置；通过蒙特卡洛事例生成与拟合分析，结合系统误差协方差矩阵，计算Wilson系数90% CL投影上限。

Result: 近探测器对ds-FCNC Wilson系数|ε_{ds}^{αβ}|（α,β=e,μ,τ）的灵敏度达3×10⁻³–2×10⁻²量级（以G_F为标度）；远探测器弱约10倍；系统误差分析表明：近探测器极限由中微子-核子本底截面不确定性主导，远探测器则由信号事例率统计+系统误差共同限制；仅含e味的系数（如ε_{ds}^{eμ}, ε_{ds}^{eτ}）的远探测器界限对δ_CP与质量序具~10%敏感性。

Conclusion: DUNE近探测器是当前最灵敏的ds-FCNC探针之一，其约束可显著压缩左/右手流型EFT参数空间；远探测器虽灵敏度较低，但提供正负号与CP相位信息互补；结果强调需提升核子结构函数与中微子核相互作用建模精度以突破系统误差瓶颈。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of DUNE-like facilities to flavor-changing
  neutral-current interactions between down and strange quarks through inclusive
  deep-inelastic neutrino scattering. In the framework of a general low-energy
  effective Lagrangian for Dirac and Majorana neutrinos, we evaluate projected
  sensitivities for CP- and $τ$-optimized beam configurations. The strongest
  bounds on the dimensionless Wilson coefficients, defined relative to the Fermi
  constant $G_F$, reach $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})\text{--}\mathcal{O}(10^{-2})$ at the
  near detector, whereas far-detector limits are roughly one order of magnitude
  weaker. When systematic uncertainties are included, the near-detector
  sensitivity is dominated by the background-rate uncertainty, while the
  far-detector sensitivity is limited primarily by the signal-rate uncertainty.
  At the far detector,
  the dependence of the projected bounds on the neutrino mass ordering and the Dirac
  CP-violating phase $δ_{\mathrm{CP}}$ is largely confined to coefficients
  involving the electron flavor.

</details>


### [101] [Embedding dependence of fermion mass hierarchies in $S_3$-symmetric Yukawa sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29259)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在S₃家族对称性下，当不可约表示以重数出现时，Yukawa耦合张量在代空间中的嵌入自由度对其奇异谱（即质量谱）的影响；特别分析了复对称三族张量的谱约束，发现嵌入参数可显著放宽质量层次限制，并指出某Clifford代数构造因固定嵌入而无法再现轻子质量层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当家族对称性导致不可约表示重数出现时，对称性仅约束Yukawa项的表示内容，却不固定其在代张量空间中的具体嵌入；这种额外自由度可能深刻影响费米子质量谱，但此前未被系统研究。

Method: 采用群表示论与代数几何方法：计算S₃在V=1⊕2上的对称平方Sym²V的分解，确定不变Yukawa子空间L_{t,κ}≅ℙ¹×ℙ¹的参数化；求解逆奇异值问题，分析秩一矩阵存在条件及σ₃/σ₂无界性；将结果应用于Clifford代数构造的显式Yukawa张量。

Result: ① 在κ=0分支上，任意正有序三族质量谱均可实现当且仅当嵌入可调；固定单态嵌入则引入有限层次上限；② L_{t,κ}含非零秩一矩阵 ⇔ 2tκ²=1，此时σ₃/σ₂无界；③ Clifford构造对应L_{2,0}，其奇异值满足m₁+m₂≤m₃≤m₁+3m₂，排除观测到的带电轻子质量比(mₑ:m_μ:m_τ)≈(0.0003:0.07:1)。

Conclusion: 费米子质量层次的可行性不仅取决于对称性表示内容，更关键地依赖于表示重数下的具体嵌入选择；固定嵌入构成独立于群论结构的物理约束，是模型构建中必须显式处理的自由度。

Abstract: When irreducible representations occur with multiplicity, a family symmetry fixes the representation content of a Yukawa sector but need not fix its embedding in the space of generation tensors. We study the spectral consequences of this additional embedding data for complex-symmetric three-family tensors with the $S_3$ assignment $V=\mathbf{1}\oplus\mathbf{2}$. Since $\operatorname{Sym}^2V=2\,\mathbf{1}\oplus2\,\mathbf{2}$, three-dimensional invariant Yukawa subspaces of type $\mathbf{1}\oplus\mathbf{2}$ form a continuous $\mathbb{CP}^1\times\mathbb{CP}^1$ family, denoted $\mathcal L_{t,κ}$ on a finite affine chart. On the $κ=0$ branch, we solve the inverse singular-value problem and obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for any prescribed ordered positive mass spectrum. A fixed singlet embedding imposes a finite hierarchy bound, whereas varying the embedding accommodates every positive three-family spectrum. For general embeddings, $\mathcal L_{t,κ}$ contains a nonzero rank-one matrix if and only if $2tκ^2=1$; equivalently, these are precisely the finite-chart embeddings for which $σ_3/σ_2$ is unbounded. In a conventional complex-symmetric $S_3$ three-Higgs sector, the same condition becomes $y_2y_5^2=\sqrt{2}\,y_1y_3^2$, so order-one Yukawa coupling ratios can support arbitrarily large mass hierarchies. We then apply the result to a recent Clifford-algebraic construction and show that its family-resolved Yukawa tensors span $\mathcal L_{2,0}$, whose singular values obey $m_1+m_2\leq m_3\leq m_1+3m_2$. This excludes the observed charged-lepton hierarchy for arbitrary complex vacuum alignment within the existing Higgs directions. The resulting obstruction is therefore tied to the fixed multiplicity embedding rather than to the $S_3$ representation content alone.

</details>


### [102] [Probing Ultra-Compressed Scotogenic Dark Matter at the HL-LHC via 4D Spacetime Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29275)
*Renjie Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用HL-LHC精密计时探测器（30 ps时间分辨率）作为第四维度观测量，针对超压缩scotogenic模型中寿命在100–1000 mm对应的70–700 ps到达延迟的带电惰性标量衰变事例，发展一种‘4D时空追踪’策略；该策略基于低p_T消失轨迹stub匹配延迟时间信号，在无背景下实现12–36%信号效率，并预计在3000 fb⁻¹数据下以95% CL排除标量质量达≈670 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LHC位移轻子与消失轨迹搜索对该模型中~2 GeV质量劈裂、cτ ~ 100–1000 mm的超压缩scotogenic参数区间覆盖不充分；而该区域因共湮灭机制可自然解释暗物质剩余丰度，却长期难以探测。

Method: 引入时间维度：利用HL-LHC精确计时探测器分辨70–700 ps延迟信号（远超SM本底的亚皮秒级到达时间），结合低横动量消失轨迹stub重建，不依赖完整轻子识别，构建四维（x,y,z,t）事件筛选策略。

Result: 在4.20×10⁶个模拟标准模型本底事例中观测到零背景；信号效率为12–36%（200 GeV基准点为14%）；在3000 fb⁻¹积分亮度、保守背景假设下，95%置信水平可排除标量质量至≈670 GeV。

Conclusion: 4D时空追踪将超压缩scotogenic共湮灭走廊从传统实验最难以触及的区域转变为HL-LHC可探测目标；小Yukawa耦合y（满足cτ ∝ y⁻²）与中微子质量约束y²λ₅的兼容性表明，该策略不仅敏感，且与辐射产生中微子质量机制自洽。

Abstract: The ultra-compressed scotogenic regime, in which a 2\,GeV mass splitting between the charged inert scalar and the dark-matter fermion is motivated by and compatible with co-annihilation to the observed relic density for suitable choices of the remaining model parameters, produces decay leptons for which existing LHC displaced-lepton and disappearing-track searches retain only partial, unoptimised acceptance, leaving room for a dedicated timing-assisted strategy. We propose exploiting the 30\,ps timing resolution of the HL-LHC precision timing detectors as a fourth observable, converting the macroscopic scalar decay length of 100--1000\,mm into an arrival-time delay of 70--700\,ps---more than three orders of magnitude above the sub-picosecond delays of all prompt Standard Model backgrounds. Operating at the level of a low-$p_T$ disappearing-track stub matched to a delayed timing hit, without requiring full lepton reconstruction, our 4D timing strategy achieves signal efficiencies of 12--36\% across the probed mass range (14\% at the 200\,GeV benchmark) and zero background across $4.20\times10^6$ simulated prompt SM events, projecting 95\%\,C.L.\ exclusion up to scalar masses of $\simeq\!670$\,GeV at 3000\,fb$^{-1}$ under conservative background assumptions. The scalar lifetime is set by the Yukawa coupling $y$ through $cτ\propto y^{-2}$, while sub-eV neutrino masses constrain only the product $y^2λ_5$; the small couplings that place the charged scalar in the optimal timing window are therefore compatible with, though not uniquely fixed by, the radiative neutrino-mass mechanism, making the co-annihilation corridor---conventionally the most elusive regime---an accessible target for 4D spacetime tracking at the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [103] [Finite-Width Dissolution of Radial Spectroscopy in Single-Top Mesonic Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29476)
*Bing-Dong Wan,Shuo Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了含一个不稳定重夸克（如顶夸克）的束缚态系统的极点宽度行为，在重质量展开下，证明其极点宽度继承组分夸克宽度，修正项为O(Λ²kin/m²Q)，而径向分裂保持O(Λrad)量级；通过非厄米Bethe–Salpeter框架与复极质量实现，验证了宽度继承的算符层面机制及m⁻²Q压低行为，并指出小宽度径向峰随宽度增大而消失，稳定顶夸克本征值仅作为参考极点存在，不形成可分辨多峰谱，但可能在Wbq末态中留下宽阈值增强等过程依赖的定性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解顶夸克作为极重且短寿命粒子在强子化前如何影响含t的准束缚态（如tq̄分子态或共振）的谱学性质与宽度传播机制，尤其在超出微扰QCD适用范围时需构造自洽非厄米有效理论。

Method: 采用瞬时Bethe–Salpeter方程框架，引入复数顶夸克极质量；利用双正交Hellmann–Feynman关系在算符层面实现宽度继承；通过人工重质量扫描验证m⁻²Q压制律；结合全宽度依赖的非厄米重对角化与全矩阵预解式直接计算，分析响应函数谱形演化。

Result: 证实极点宽度在O(Λ²kin/m²Q)精度下严格继承单体宽度；tq̄（q = b,c,u）通道的低极点源投影响应呈现单一宽峰，峰值位于物理顶夸克宽度处；随宽度增大，原小宽度下的径向激发峰逐渐弥散消失；稳定顶夸克本征值退化为不可分辨的参考极点，不再构成离散多峰谱。

Conclusion: 顶夸克的极端质量-宽度比（m_t/Γ_t ≈ 1.5）导致传统径向激发谱坍缩，其强子化前动力学由宽度主导而非能级分裂；观测上不预期清晰的tq̄共振峰结构，而应关注Wbq末态中的宽阈值效应、色流修改等间接动力学印记。

Abstract: Within the heavy-mass expansion, the pole width of a system containing one unstable heavy constituent inherits the constituent width up to $\mathcal O(Λ_{\rm kin}^2/m_Q^2)$ corrections, while radial splittings remain $\mathcal O(Λ_{\rm rad})$. The top quark is an extreme realization of this hierarchy. We implement the complex top pole mass in an instantaneous Bethe--Salpeter framework, where a biorthogonal Hellmann--Feynman relation realizes width inheritance at the operator level and an artificial heavy-mass scan confirms the predicted $m_Q^{-2}$ suppression. The low-pole source-projected response has a single broad maximum at the physical top width in the $t\bar b$, $t\bar c$, and $t\bar u$ channels. Full width-dependent non-Hermitian re-diagonalization and a direct full-matrix resolvent evaluation confirm the progressive dissolution of the small-width radial maxima. Thus stable-top eigenvalues survive as reference poles but not as a resolvable multi-peak spectrum; they may instead leave qualitative, process-dependent $Wb\bar q$ signatures, such as a broad threshold enhancement or modified color flow.

</details>


### [104] [Probability-based Estimates of the Uncalculated N$^5$LO QCD Contribution to the Hadronic $W$-Boson Decay Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29666)
*Shu-Heng Yang,Jiang Yan,Xing-Gang Wu,Zhi-Fei Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用贝叶斯分析（BA）结合最大共形性原理（PMC）估计W玻色子强子衰变宽度的N⁵LO QCD修正，得到QCD修正不确定度±1.0×10⁻⁵，最终强子分支比为(65.84±1.54)%，与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N⁵LO QCD修正因计算复杂尚未精确获得，需发展可靠外推方法以提升理论预言精度，并缩小与实验比较的不确定性。

Method: 首先应用PMC消除初始微扰级数中的非共形项，获得方案无关、尺度不变且收敛性更优的级数；再以该改进级数为输入，采用贝叶斯分析估计N⁵LO贡献及其可信区间。

Result: N⁵LO QCD修正的BA 95.5%可信区间不确定度为Δδ_QCD|PMC,BA^N⁵LO = ±1.0×10⁻⁵；PMC下W→hadrons分支比为(65.84±1.54)%。

Conclusion: PMC+BA联合策略为高阶微扰QCD不可算项提供了可控、可靠且具物理意义的估计方法，显著提升了W衰变宽度的理论精度，强化了标准模型检验基础。

Abstract: The perturbative QCD corrections to the hadronic decay width of the $W$ boson are currently known up to next-to-next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order ($\mathrm{N^4LO}$), whereas the exact $\mathrm{N^5LO}$ correction remains unavailable owing to its formidable computational complexity. In this work, we estimate the $\mathrm{N^5LO}$ contributions by adopting Bayesian analysis (BA). Before performing the estimation, the Principle of Maximum Conformality (PMC) is employed to improve the precision of the initial scale-dependent perturbative series. Through recursive application of the renormalization group equation, non-conformal terms are absorbed into the strong running coupling, yielding a scheme-independent, scale-invariant perturbative series with improved convergence. The PMC procedure determines an effective coupling $α_s(Q_*)$, with the PMC scale fixed as $Q_* = 100.102~\mathrm{GeV}$ at next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy. Based on the improved and more precise series, the $95.5\%$ BA credible interval yields an uncertainty of $Δδ_\mathrm{QCD}|_{\mathrm{PMC, BA}}^{\mathrm{N^5LO}} = \pm 1.0\times 10^{-5}$. The resulting hadronic branching ratio is $\mathcal{B}(W\to \text{hadrons})|_\mathrm{PMC} = (65.84\pm 1.54)\%$, which is consistent with experimental data within reasonable errors.

</details>


### [105] [On-shell renormalization in NREFT: a simplified description of resonances, bound states, and coupled channel effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29683)
*Guo-Ying Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了Weinberg复合性判据的现代推广，指出非相对论有效场论（NREFT）中的壳上重整化可复现Weinberg结果，并简洁描述共振态、束缚态及耦合通道效应；同时给出含带电通道的近阈p波态完整传播子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Weinberg复合性判据以适用于更一般的非相对论强相互作用系统，尤其包含多通道耦合与带电粒子的物理情形。

Method: 采用非相对论有效场论（NREFT）框架，结合壳上（on-shell）重整化方案，系统分析近阈p波态的传播子结构，显式纳入带电通道的库仑修正。

Result: 证明NREFT壳上重整化严格复现Weinberg原始复合性参数；导出含带电通道的完整p波传播子，统一处理共振、束缚态及多通道耦合效应。

Conclusion: Weinberg复合性判据可通过NREFT壳上重整化自然推广，为阈值附近强子分子态与散射态的定量判别提供了普适、自洽且可计算的理论工具。

Abstract: We discuss several issues regarding the modern generalization of Weinberg's compositeness criterion. In particular, we demonstrate that the on-shell renormalization in NREFT reproduces Weinberg's result and provides a simple description of resonances, bound states, and coupled channel effects. Additionally, we present the complete propagator, including the charged channel for the near-threshold p-wave states.

</details>


### [106] [$(g-2)_{\ell}$ and LFV decays in a $U(1)_{L_μ -L_τ}$ left-right model with inverse seesaw neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29697)
*L. T. Hue,Vo Quoc Phong,T. D. Tham,N. H. T. Nha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ} 左右对称模型中，利用逆向跷跷板机制生成中微子质量，并通过单重带电希格斯玻色子的一圈图贡献解释电子与缪子的反常磁矩偏差；同时，由于U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}规范对称性，所有带电流轻子味破坏过程（如h→e_b e_a, Z→e_b e_a, e_b→e_aγ）均被高度压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释缪子与电子反常磁矩（g−2）实验与标准模型预测之间的微小但显著偏差，同时保持轻子味破坏过程在当前实验极限内可接受。

Method: 在具有U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}规范对称性和逆向跷跷板中微子质量机制的左右对称模型框架下，计算单重带电希格斯玻色子交换的一圈费曼图对(g−2)_e和(g−2)_μ的贡献，并系统分析LFV衰变h→ℓ_iℓ_j、Z→ℓ_iℓ_j及ℓ_j→ℓ_iγ的振幅压制机制。

Result: 单重带电希格斯玻色子质量在TeV量级、耦合O(0.1)时，可分别产生Δa_μ ∼ 10^{−9}与Δa_e ∼ O(10^{−13})的贡献，匹配观测偏差；所有LFV过程因U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}规范对称性导致的荷选择规则而自然压低至远低于当前实验灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该模型在不违背现有LFV实验约束的前提下，为(g−2)_e和(g−2)_μ提供统一且自洽的超出标准模型解释，凸显U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}对称性在协调轻子反常与味物理中的关键作用。

Abstract: In the framework of a $U(1)_{L_μ -L_τ}$ left-right model with inverse seesaw neutrinos recently proposed, we show that one-loop contributions from heavy singly charged Higgs bosons to the anomalous magnetic moments of charged leptons $(g-2)_{e,μ}$ can accommodate the observed discrepancies, of $10^{-9}$ and $\mathcal{O}(10^{-13})$ for the muon and electron, respectively. Meanwhile, all lepton-flavor violating decay rates for $h\to e_b^\pm e_a^\mp $, $Z\to e_b^\pm e_a^\mp$, and $e_b\to e_a γ$ are found to be highly suppressed as a consequence of the gauged $U(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}$ symmetry.

</details>


### [107] [X(2370) as the pseudoscalar glueball: Another clue from a constituent approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29775)
*F. Buisseret*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于通量管能量的唯象模型，将强子衰变宽度线性关联于其质量，并成功解释X(2370)作为赝标胶球候选者的质量和衰宽；该模型亦支持f₂(2150)和f₂(2300)为张量胶球候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上X(2370)是最清晰的赝标胶球候选者，但需理论模型统一描述其质量与衰变宽度，并鉴别其他胶球候选态。

Method: 采用双横动量组分胶子加伴随表示通量管的组分模型；假设胶球或轻无味介子总衰变宽度正比于通量管所储能量，建立衰宽–质量线性关系；先用轻无味介子拟合，再外推至胶球。

Result: X(2370)的质量与衰宽均与组分模型预测一致；f₂(2150)与f₂(2300)被识别为有希望的张量胶球候选者。

Conclusion: 该通量管能量驱动的唯象模型能自洽地统一描述轻介子与胶球的质量-衰宽关系，为胶球鉴别提供新判据。

Abstract: The pseudoscalar state X(2370) is the clearest glueball candidate experimentally identified so far. From a phenomenological point of view, constituent approaches, in which the lightest glueballs are modelled as bound states of two transverse constituent gluons linked by an adjoint flux tube, reach a good agreement with quenched lattice QCD pure gauge spectrum. Based on the assumption that the total decay width of glueballs or light unflavoured mesons is proportional to the energy stored in the flux tube, a model is developed that linearly correlates the hadron decay width to its mass. The model is first fitted on light unflavoured mesons and then extended to glueballs. Both the mass and decay width of the X(2370) are compatible with the results of the constituent approach. Other experimental glueball candidates in the scalar and tensor channels are also reviewed to show the ability of the developed approach to identify glueballs: The f$_2$(2150) and f$_2$(2300) states appear as promising tensor glueball candidates.

</details>


### [108] [Seesaw and Axion in No-Scale Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29801)
*Anamaria Hell,Lincoln da S. Pereira,Tsutomu T. Yanagida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了No-Scale引力理论中Jordan与Einstein帧的经典等价性问题，指出排除标量场φ=0这一奇点后，两帧可保持经典等价；Jordan帧更清晰展现模型对称性，有利于粒子物理构造，如高质量轴子和自然实现跷跷板机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决No-Scale引力中Jordan与Einstein帧等价性的长期争议，尤其澄清奇点φ=0对经典等价性的影响，并挖掘Jordan帧在对称性呈现与粒子物理建模中的优势。

Method: 通过严格分析标量-张量理论中共形变换的定义域，排除φ=0奇点，建立两帧间非奇异、可逆的经典映射；继而分析Jordan帧中作用量的对称结构，推导离散对称性及相应粒子物理现象。

Result: 证明在排除φ=0后，No-Scale理论的Jordan与Einstein帧存在经典等价；Jordan帧显式呈现全局/局域对称性，由此导出保护轴子质量的离散Z_N对称性，并使右手中微子质量项自然出现，实现Type-I seesaw机制。

Conclusion: Jordan帧不仅是等价的描述，更是更富物理信息的框架；No-Scale结构在无精细调节下可同时解释轴子暗物质与中微子质量，凸显其作为统一框架的潜力。

Abstract: The No-Scale gravity is a compelling framework to describe particle physics, gravity and cosmology, in which all mass scales are an illusion given by the value of a scalar field $φ$. This theory, however, falls under the umbrella of more general scalar-tensor theories, which propagate different modes depending on the scalar field, and faces the extensive debate regarding the equivalence between its Jordan and Einstein frame descriptions. In this work we advocate that by excluding the singular point in the transformation between the two frames (e.g., $φ=0$) we can have the classical equivalence between them. To show the merit of removing this singular point, we argue the Jordan frame makes the symmetries of the model manifest, which creates a suitable playground for the particle-physics model building which we illustrate by showing the emergence of discrete symmetries which leads to a high-quality axion and also naturally incorporate the seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [109] [Meson gravitational D-form factors and symmetry breaking in low-energy QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30434)
*Mamiya Kawaguchi,Kazuhiro Tanaka,Mitsuru Tanaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在三味线性σ模型中研究介子的引力D-型因子，揭示其与低能QCD对称性破缺的深刻联系；通过标量介子主导图像和手征极限分析，得到赝标量与标量介子D-型因子的解析表达式，并指出U(1)_A反常对标量及单态赝标量介子D-型因子的修正；现实味破缺下，低于1 GeV的标量介子质量谱相互作用显著影响其D-型因子；与含标度子场的手征微扰论对比表明，差异源于质量维数不同的相互作用项；D-型因子是探测低能强子物理对称性破缺结构的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低能QCD中对称性破缺机制，特别是手征对称性与标度对称性的实现方式，需借助介子在引力耦合下的形状信息——即D-型因子；该量编码能量-动量张量分布，对对称性破缺模式高度敏感。

Method: 采用三味线性σ模型框架，结合标量介子主导（SMD）图像，推导赝标量与标量介子在手征极限与三味对称极限下的D-型因子解析表达式；引入显式味破缺相互作用以再现标量介子质量谱（如f₀(500), K⁎₀(700), a₀(980), f₀(980)）；并与含标度子场的χPT Lagrangian结果进行系统比较，依据算符质量维数判别贡献主导机制。

Result: 在三味对称极限下获得赝标量八重态与标量介子D-型因子的解析形式；手征极限中，八重态赝标量D-型因子前向极限值为−1；U(1)_A反常导致单态赝标量及标量介子D-型因子出现非平凡修正；味破缺相互作用显著调制标量介子D-型因子幅值与q²依赖；与含标度子χPT的差异源于质量维数d=4（σ模型）与d<4（χPT）项在介子质量生成中的不同角色。

Conclusion: 介子D-型因子是低能强子物理中区分手征对称性破缺、U(1)_A反常与标度对称性破缺机制的关键可观测量；其对模型中对称性实现方式（如标量场是否为手征四极矩或标度子）具有高度选择性，可为Lattice QCD与未来引力探针实验提供理论基准。

Abstract: We investigate meson gravitational D-form factors in the three-flavor linear sigma model and their connection to symmetry breaking in low-energy QCD. Within the scalar meson dominance picture, we examine the scalar meson exchange contributions and their relation to meson masses through scalar-meson couplings. In the three-flavor symmetric limit, we first derive analytical expressions for the D-form factors of pseudoscalar and scalar mesons. In the chiral limit, the forward-limit value of the octet pseudoscalar D-form factor is fixed to $-1$, whereas the $U(1)_A$ anomaly generates additional contributions to the singlet pseudoscalar and scalar-meson D-form factors. For realistic flavor breaking, the interaction introduced to reproduce the scalar-meson mass hierarchy below $1$ GeV significantly affects the scalar-meson D-form factors. We further compare the linear sigma model results with those obtained from a chiral perturbation theory Lagrangian including a dilatonic scalar field and show that their differences are governed by the canonical mass dimensions of the interaction terms contributing to meson mass generation. These results indicate that meson D-form factors provide sensitive probes of symmetry-breaking structures in low-energy hadron physics.

</details>


### [110] [Debye screening mass in hot QCD at three loops: Canonical form of the integrand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29844)
*York Schröder,Miguel Váez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了热QCD中三圈阶德拜屏蔽质量的计算策略，利用热求和被积函数的规范形式与积分变量平移技巧，以系统化处理大量费曼图贡献，并为更高阶计算提供简化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德拜屏蔽质量是表征高温夸克胶子等离子体中色电场屏蔽效应的基本参数，在热场论、输运现象、早期宇宙重粒子相互作用及维数约化程序中具有核心地位；然而其三圈阶计算面临被积函数项数爆炸问题，亟需更高效的组织策略。

Method: 采用基于热（求和）被积函数规范形式的最新技术，结合积分变量平移（integration variable shifts）对三圈阶德拜质量贡献进行系统性约化与分类。

Result: 确立了一套可推广的三圈阶德拜质量计算框架，显著降低了代数复杂度，并揭示了规范表示在高阶热QCD计算中的结构简化潜力。

Conclusion: 规范形式与变量平移是组织高阶热QCD计算的关键工具，该策略不仅适用于德拜质量，亦可推广至其他热力学量与输运系数的多圈计算。

Abstract: The Debye screening mass is a fundamental parameter characterizing the screening of chromo-electric fields in a hot quark-gluon plasma. It plays an important role in thermal field theory, entering the description of screening lengths, transport phenomena, heavy-particle interactions in the early Universe, and the dimensional reduction program that connects thermal QCD to effective three-dimensional theories. In this contribution, we revisit the reduction strategy that serves to organize the large number of terms that contribute to the screening mass at the three-loop level, applying recent advances based on canonical forms of thermal (sum-) integrands. We emphasize the role of integration variable shifts, and discuss how canonical representations of thermal integrands may simplify future high-order calculations in hot QCD.

</details>


### [111] [Kaon gravitational form factors and mechanical structure in a three-flavor NJL model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29848)
*Zhibo Liu,Hiroaki Abuki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型中，采用固有时间正则化方法研究了K介子的引力形状因子（GFFs），通过构造满足引力Ward-Takahashi恒等式的 dressed quark–graviton 顶点，分别提取轻夸克与奇异夸克对GFFs的贡献，并对比π介子结果；发现由于显式味对称性破缺，奇异夸克主导动量与力学响应，导致K介子D项负值更小、力学分布更紧凑、中心压强与剪切力更强；同时强调C项投影的WTI保持约化对恢复π介子低能定理至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子内部质量、自旋与机械结构的量子色动力学起源，特别是介子层次上味对称性破缺对引力形状因子及力学分布的影响；需在非微扰有效理论中严格满足引力守恒律（即引力Ward-Takahashi恒等式）。

Method: 采用proper-time-regularized three-flavor NJL模型，将K介子视为轻-奇赝标束缚态；计算其能量-动量张量矩阵元，引入dressed quark–graviton顶点，同时保留接触相互作用项与夸克三角图，以保证总能量-动量张量守恒；通过投影算符分离GFFs（A, B, C, D），并计算压力、剪切力分布及Breit帧与光前横向质量半径。

Result: K介子中奇异夸克贡献显著大于轻夸克，导致其D-term > D_π（即负值更小）；压力与剪切力分布更局域化，中心压强与最大剪切力均增强；质量半径小于π介子；C-term的WTI保持约化是恢复π介子低能定理（如θ_μν的迹反常匹配）的必要条件。

Conclusion: K介子的引力结构由显式SU(2)_L×SU(2)_R破缺主导，其力学性质比π介子更紧凑、更刚性；NJL模型在proper-time正则化下可自洽描述介子GFFs，但C-term处理必须尊重引力Ward-Takahashi恒等式，否则破坏手征低能定理。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational form factors (GFFs) of the kaon in a
  proper-time-regularized three-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model. We treat the kaon as a light--strange pseudoscalar bound state and evaluate
  its energy--momentum-tensor matrix element using a dressed quark--graviton vertex. By retaining the interaction-induced contact contribution alongside the
  quark triangle diagrams, we preserve the gravitational Ward--Takahashi
  identity and ensure the conservation of the total energy--momentum tensor. We determine the light- and strange-quark contributions to the kaon GFFs,
  contrast them with the corresponding pion
  results, and extract the associated pressure and shear-force distributions,
  together with the Breit-frame and transverse light-front mass radii. Explicit flavor-symmetry breaking dictates that the strange sector
  carries a larger share of the kaon momentum and mechanical response. Consequently, compared to the pion, the kaon exhibits a less negative
  $D$-term and more compact mechanical distributions, featuring enhanced
  central pressure and shear. We further show that a Ward--Takahashi-identity-preserving reduction
  of the $C$-term projection is essential for maintaining the pion
  low-energy theorem in proper-time regularization.

</details>


### [112] [From Threshold Crossing to Wave-function Renormalization: Defining the Pion Mott Temperature in a Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31125)
*Sidney S. Avancini,Maximo Coppola,Dyana C. Duarte,Ricardo L. S. Farias,Norberto N. Scoccola,William R. Tavares*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在热磁化夸克物质中中性π介子的解离行为，利用两味Nambu–Jona-Lasinio模型，提出以π⁰波函数重整化因子Z_{π⁰}(T,eB)的拐点定义磁Mott温度，表明π介子解离是一种谱交叉而非简单阈值穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强磁场下，传统基于质量-阈值比较（m_{π⁰}=2M）的Mott温度定义失效，因朗道量子化导致多重连续谱与多个极点解，需建立适用于有限磁场的物理清晰的Mott温度判据。

Method: 采用两味NJL模型，在有限温T与外加磁场eB下求解π⁰介子传播子的极点方程；引入波函数重整化因子Z_{π⁰}，以其对温度的一阶导数极值（即拐点）定义磁Mott温度T_{Mott}(eB)。

Result: 发现Z_{π⁰}(T,eB)的拐点温度T^{inf}(eB)光滑连接零场极限下的传统Mott温度，并与手征赝临界温度T_{pc}(eB)高度一致（尤其在常耦合与磁场依赖耦合两种方案下）；证实π⁰谱强度衰减是连续的谱交叉过程。

Conclusion: 磁Mott温度应由π⁰谱函数强度衰减速率的最大值（即Z_{π⁰}的拐点）定义，该定义兼具物理意义与连续性，揭示了强磁场下介子解离的本质是渐进式谱重分布而非 abrupt 阈值消失。

Abstract: We investigate the dissociation of the neutral pion in hot magnetized quark matter within the two-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model. At zero magnetic field, the Mott temperature is conventionally determined by $m_{π^0}(T_{\rm Mott})=2M(T_{\rm Mott})$, above which a real pole ceases to exist. At finite magnetic field, Landau quantization replaces this single threshold by a hierarchy of quark--antiquark continua and generates multiple solutions of the pion pole equation, rendering a direct threshold-crossing criterion ambiguous since a real pion solution below the lowest nominal threshold always exists. We therefore propose to define the magnetic Mott temperature through the inflection point of the pion wave-function renormalization factor $Z_{π^0}(T,eB)$ of that lowest pole, corresponding to the fastest loss of its spectral function strength. The prescription reproduces the conventional Mott temperature as $eB\to 0$ and tracks the chiral pseudocritical temperature for both constant and magnetic-field-dependent couplings. Our results characterize pion dissociation in a magnetic field as a spectral crossover rather than a simple threshold crossing.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用介质中矢量介子（特别是φ介子）的质量变化是探测量子色动力学（QCD）手征对称性部分恢复及介质效应的关键问题；横向与纵向极化模式的区分对于刻画各向异性响应和动量依赖的谱函数至关重要。

Method: 采用有限温度QCD求和规则（finite-temperature QCD sum rules），在热平衡、μ_B=0条件下，对φ介子流关联函数的横向（T）与纵向（L）投影分别构建求和规则，并通过Borel变换提取有效质量，考虑算符乘积展开（OPE）至维度六算符及非微扰真空凝聚项的温度依赖行为。

Result: 发现φ介子的横向与纵向有效质量均随温度升高而减小，但减小速率不同；二者之差（即T–L质量劈裂）随温度和空间动量单调增大，在中等动量（p ∼ 1 GeV）和接近Tc时可达数十MeV量级。

Conclusion: φ介子在热介质中表现出显著的极化依赖质量修正，其横向–纵向质量劈裂是QCD介质中矢量介子传播各向异性的直接体现，可作为重离子碰撞实验中手征恢复与介质极化效应的潜在观测量。

Abstract: We analyze the in-medium modification of the $φ$ meson mass below the pseudo-critical temperature $T_\text{c}$ at vanishing baryon chemical potential using QCD sum rules. The sum rules are applied separately to the transverse and longitudinal polarization modes of the $φ$ meson defined relative to the spatial momentum in the medium rest frame. We map out the temperature and momentum dependence of the mass in each mode and quantify the resulting transverse--longitudinal mass splitting. The splitting develops with increasing temperature and momentum.

</details>


### [114] [The inclusive $\bar B \to X_s γ$ decay rate with higher precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29863)
*M. Misiak,H. M. Asatrian,K. Brune,M. Czaja,M. Czakon,M. Fael,C. Greub,T. Huber,F. Lange,L. T. Moos,M. Niggetiedt,A. Rehman,K. Schönwald,M. Steinhauser*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对B介子的含光子弱衰变过程进行了升级版的理论分析，包含了完整的O(α_s)修正和部分O(α_s²)修正（含精确m_c依赖），并采用最新HQE参数（来自含O(α_s³)的半轻子拟合），给出标准模型预言B→X_sγ分支比为(3.54±0.14)×10⁻⁴（E_γ>1.6 GeV），与实验一致，并由此对2HDM-II模型中带电Higgs质量给出95% CL下限670 GeV。
